of approximately RMB1,192.7 million; gross profit margin was          本 集 團 實 現 收 益 約 為 人 民 幣 11.927 億 元；

     主席報告


     In terms of residential property management services, amid                在住宅物業管理服務方面，面對宏觀經濟環
     adjustments in the macroeconomic environment and industry                 境與行業形勢的調整，本集團始終以審慎穩
     landscape, the Group has constantly pursued development with a            健的姿態尋求發展，主動調整營運規模及市

                                                MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
                                                                   管理層討論及分析


In 2025, the commercial landscape underwent profound                         二零二五年，商業環境在深度變革中演進。「Z
transformation. “Generation Z” has gradually emerged as                      世代」逐步成為消費主導群體，其消費理念、
the dominant consumer group, with significant shifts in their                喜 好 內 容、購 買 渠 道、傳 播 方 式 等 都 發 生 顯
consumption philosophies, content preferences, purchasing channels,          著變化，行業已進入購物商場超量發展後的
and communication methods. The industry has entered a phase of               調整與資源整合階段，同時品牌開店策略也
adjustment and resource consolidation following the era of excessive         轉向更注重發展質量和投資效率。面對這一
shopping mall development. At the same time, brand expansion                 趨 勢，商 業 營 運 者 必 須 應 勢 而 變，在 認 知 上
strategies have shifted towards prioritising quality and investment          率先迭代。
efficiency. In response to this trend, commercial operators must adapt
proactively to the evolving environment and take the lead in cognitive
iteration.


Thus, the core of commercial property management lies in                     由此，商業物業管理的核心在於因地制宜，
localisation, implementation of the “Store-specific Policies” principle,     「一店一策」及提升差異化競爭力。本集團的
and enhancement of differentiated competitiveness. The Group’s               經營策略聚焦四大方向：一 . 動態研判本地消
operational strategy focuses on four key directions: (1) dynamically         費趨勢，明確項目定位；二 . 加強前置招商規
analyse local consumption trends to clearly define project positioning;      劃，依照項目定位、坪效邏輯制定招商策略；
(2) refine pre-leasing planning and formulate tenant sourcing                三 . 堅持深度運營與精準營銷，重視並激活會
strategies based on project positioning and sales maximisation               員資產；四 . 推進降本增效，提升業務運營效
logic; (3) persist with in-depth operations and implement targeted           率。與 此 同 時，營 運 團 隊 需 要 重 點 提 升 本 集
marketing initiatives, with a focus on optimising and leveraging             團以下三大能力：洞察市場變化與快速解題
membership assets; (4) advance cost reduction and efficiency                 應變的能力、積極爭取資源與帶領團隊攻堅
enhancement to improve overall business operational efficiency.              破局的能力及規劃未來項目可持續良性發展
Meanwhile, the operations team must focus on strengthening three             的 能 力。商 業 經 營 要 以 終 為 始，錨 定 目 標，
core competencies of the Group: the ability to discern market shifts         找到並解決那些能改變項目命運、具備自我

     AUDITOR’S REMUNERATION                                                     核數師酬金

     For the year under review, the fees paid to the auditor of the             回顧年內，就審計服務及非審計服務而向本
     Company, Prism Hong Kong Limited (“Prism”), in respect of the audit        公司核數師栢淳會計師事務所有限公司（「栢
     services and non-audit services provided to the Company amounted           淳」）支付的酬金分別是約人民幣 90 萬元及約
     to approximately RMB0.9 million and approximately RMB0.2 million,          人民幣 20 萬元。非審計服務主要為審閱本集
     respectively. Non-audit services mainly represent the review of the        團截至二零二五年六月三十日止六個月的中
     Group’s interim results announcement and interim report for the six        期業績公告及中期報告。
     months ended 30 June 2025.

INTERNAL CONTROLS                                                          內部監控

The Board is fully responsible for maintaining proper and effective        董事會全面負責維持妥善而有效的內部監控
internal controls and for regularly reviewing the operational efficiency   系 統，定 期 檢 討 該 系 統 財 務、營 運、合 規、
of the financial, operational, compliance, risk controls, human            風 險 控 制、人 力 資 源 等 方 面 的 運 作 效 率，以
resources and other aspects of the system in order to safeguard the        保障本集團職責與權力的各自獨立，繼而維
independence of the respective duties and powers of the Group              護股東之投資及本公司之資產。
which in turn can protect the investment of shareholders and the
assets of the Company.


The Internal Audit and Compliance Department of the Company,               本公司審計監察部（即為本公司的內審部門）
being the Company’s internal audit function, is responsible for regular    負責定期對本公司及附屬公司進行財務及營
review and audit of the finance and operation of the Company and           運審閱以及審計。該部門此項工作的目的在
its subsidiaries for the purpose of ensuring the internal controls are     於確保內部監控正常運作並發揮其應有的作
in place and functioning properly as intended. For weaknesses of           用。對於外聘核數師向本公司報告其監測到
internal controls and accounting procedures of the Group which the         的本集團內部監控及會計程序的不足之處，
external auditor has identified and reported to the Company, the           本公司充份重視所提建議，並作出相應改進。
Company would pay full attention to the recommendations made by
the external auditor and make appropriate improvements.


During the year under review, the Board has conducted a review             於回顧年內，董事會已不時對本集團風險管
of the risk management and internal control systems of the Group           理及內部監控系統及其有效性進行檢討，並
and their effectiveness from time to time and concluded that the           認為年度內本集團風險管理及內部監控系統
risk management and internal control systems of the Group were             及其有效性足夠高效。
adequate and effective during the Year.


The Company has the procedures and internal controls for the               本公司設有關於處理及發佈內幕消息的程序
handling and dissemination of inside information. In practice,             及 內 部 監 控。於 實 行 時，當 本 集 團 僱 員 得 悉
employees of the Group who become aware of any events and/                 任何事件及╱或事宜被其視之為潛在內幕消
or matters which he/she considers potentially inside information,          息，該 僱 員 將 向 本 公 司 指 定 人 員 匯 報，而 倘
will report to the designated personnel of the Company who, if             有關人員認為適宜，彼將向董事會提呈有關
considered appropriate, will pass such information to the Board            消息以供考慮及決定有關消息是否構成內幕
for the purpose of considering and deciding whether or not such            消息並須即時披露。
information constitutes inside information and disclosure of which
shall be made immediately.


The aforementioned risk management and internal control systems            前述風險管理和內部控制系統旨在管理而不
are designed to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to        是消除未能實現業務目標的風險，並且只能
achieve business objectives, and can only provide reasonable and not       針對重大錯誤陳述或損失提供合理而非絕對
absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss.                  的保證。

CONFIRMATIONS FROM THE COMPANY’S                                           本公司獨立核數師之確認

REMUNERATION POLICIES AND DIRECTORS’                                        薪酬政策及董事酬金
REMUNERATION

The Remuneration Committee oversees the overall remuneration                薪酬委員會監督本集團之整體薪酬政策及結
policy and structure of the Group. The Group provides employees             構。本集團向僱員提供具競爭力的薪酬待遇，
with competitive remuneration and benefits. The remuneration                並按僱員的表現及貢獻以及行業薪酬水平定
policy is reviewed on a regular basis based on the performance and          期檢討薪酬政策。
contribution of the employees and the industry remuneration level.


The authority of the Board to determine directors’ fees are subject         董事會釐定董事袍金的授權須於股東大會上
to shareholders’ approval at general meetings. The emoluments               獲股東批准。應付董事酬金由本公司董事會
payable to the Directors are determined by the Company’s Board with         參考董事職責、責任及表現以及本集團業績
reference to Directors’ duties, responsibilities and performance and        後釐定。
the results of the Group.

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the         我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不

can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,               或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總

consolidated financial statements.                                          陳述可被視作重大。

      2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                     2.1 編製基準

            These financial statements have been prepared in accordance              該等財務報表乃根據香港會計師公會
            with HKFRS Accounting Standards (which include all Hong                 （「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的香港財務報
            Kong Financial Reporting Standards, Hong Kong Accounting                 告準則會計準則（包括所有香港財務報
            Standards (“HKASs”) and Interpretations) as issued by the Hong           告準則會計準則、香港會計準則（「香港
            Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”) and            會計準則」
                                                                                         ）及詮釋）及香港公司條例的
            the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies                   披露規定編製。其亦按歷史成本慣例編
            Ordinance. They have been prepared under the historical                  製。除另有註明外，該等財務報表均以
            cost convention. These financial statements are presented in             人民幣（「人民幣」）呈列，而當中所有金
            Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded to the nearest               額均約整至最接近的千位數（「人民幣千
            thousand (“RMB’000”) except when otherwise indicated.                    元」）。


            Basis of consolidation                                                   合併基準

            The consolidated financial statements include the financial              綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司
            statements of the Company and its subsidiaries for the year              截至二零二五年十二月三十一日止年度
            ended 31 December 2025.                                                  之財務報表。


            A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity), directly      附屬公司為本公司直接或間接控制之實
            or indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control is achieved            體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團對參與
            when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns            投資對象業務的浮動回報承擔風險或享
            from its involvement with the investee and has the ability to            有權利以及能透過對投資對象之權力（即
            affect those returns through its power over the investee (i.e.,          本集團獲賦予現有能力以主導投資對象
            existing rights that give the Group the current ability to direct        相關活動之既存權利）影響該等回報時，
            the relevant activities of the investee).                                即取得控制權。

      to the net assets of the joint venture. Joint control is the              淨資產享有權利。共同控制指在合約上
      contractually agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,                同意分享安排的控制權，這僅在關於相
      which exists only when decisions about the relevant activities            關活動的決定須獲分享控制權各方一致
      require the unanimous consent of the parties sharing control.             同意時存在。

            Goodwill is initially measured at cost, being the excess of             商譽初始按成本計量，即已轉讓代價、
            the aggregate of the consideration transferred, the amount              已確認非控股權益的金額及本集團先前
            recognised for non-controlling interests and any fair value of the      持有的任何被收購方股權的公允值總
            Group’s previously held equity interests in the acquiree over the       額，超過所收購可識別資產及所承擔負
            identifiable assets acquired and liabilities assumed. If the sum of     債的差額。如該代價與其他項目的總額
            this consideration and other items is lower than the fair value         低於所收購資產淨值的公允值，該差額
            of the net assets acquired, the difference is, after reassessment,      經重新評估後於損益內確認為議價收購
            recognised in profit or loss as a gain on bargain purchase.             的收益。


            After initial recognition, goodwill is measured at cost less            初始確認後，商譽按成本減去任何累計
            any accumulated impairment losses. Goodwill is tested for               減 值 虧 損 計 量。商 譽 須 每 年 作 減 值 測
            impairment annually or more frequently if events or changes in          試，若有事件發生或情況改變顯示賬面
            circumstances indicate that the carrying value may be impaired.         值可能出現減值時，則會更頻密地進行
            The Group performs its annual impairment test of goodwill as at         測試。本集團於十二月三十一日進行商
            31 December. For the purpose of impairment testing, goodwill            譽的年度減值測試。為進行減值測試，
            acquired in a business combination is, from the acquisition             業務合併中所收購的商譽自收購日期起
            date, allocated to each of the Group’s cash-generating units, or        被分配至預期可從合併協同效應中獲益
            groups of cash-generating units, that are expected to benefit           的本集團各現金產生單位或現金產生單
            from the synergies of the combination, irrespective of whether          位組別，而無論本集團其他資產或負債
            other assets or liabilities of the Group are assigned to those          是否已分配至該等單位或單位組別。
            units or groups of units.


            Impairment is determined by assessing the recoverable amount            減值乃通過評估與商譽有關的現金產生
            of the cash-generating unit (group of cash-generating units) to         單位（現金產生單位組別）的可收回金額
            which the goodwill relates. Where the recoverable amount of             釐 定。倘 現 金 產 生 單 位（現 金 產 生 單 位
            the cash-generating unit (group of cash-generating units) is less       組 別）的 可 收 回 金 額 低 於 賬 面 值，則 確
            than the carrying amount, an impairment loss is recognised.             認減值虧損。已就商譽確認的減值虧損
            An impairment loss recognised for goodwill is not reversed in a         不得於隨後期間撥回。
            subsequent period.


            Where goodwill has been allocated to a cash-generating unit             倘商譽已分配至現金產生單位（或現金
            (or group of cash-generating units) and part of the operation           產生單位組別）而該單位的部分業務被
            within that unit is disposed of, the goodwill associated with the       出售，則在釐定出售收益或虧損時，與
            operation disposed of is included in the carrying amount of the         所出售業務相關的商譽會計入該業務的

            Goodwill disposed of in these circumstances is measured based           根據所出售業務的相對價值及現金產生

2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                  2.4 重大會計政策（續）

      Fair value measurement                                                         公允值計量

      Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset                公允值乃市場參與者於計量日期進行的
      or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between              有序交易中出售資產所收取或轉讓負債
      market participants at the measurement date. The fair value                    所支付的價格。公允值計量乃基於假設
      measurement is based on the presumption that the transaction                   出售資產或轉讓負債的交易於該資產或
      to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in the          負債的主要市場或（在未有主要市場的
      principal market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of              情況下）於該資產或負債的最有利市場
      a principal market, in the most advantageous market for the                    進行。主要或最有利市場須為本集團可
      asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market              進入的市場。資產或負債的公允值乃基
      must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset                    於市場參與者為資產或負債定價所用的
      or a liability is measured using the assumptions that market                   假設計量（假設市場參與者依照彼等的
      participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,                    最佳經濟利益行事）。
      assuming that market participants act in their economic best
      interest.


      A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into                   非金融資產的公允值計量須考慮市場參
      account a market participant’s ability to generate economic                    與者能自最大限度使用該資產達致最佳
      benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by                  用途的能力，或將該資產售予另一能自
      selling it to another market participant that would use the asset              最大限度使用該資產達致最佳用途的其
      in its highest and best use.                                                   他市場參與者，所產生的經濟效益。


      The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in                    本集團採用適用於有關情況並可獲得充
      the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to               足數據以供計量公允值的估值方法，以
      measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable                  盡量使用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減
      inputs and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                          少使用不可觀察輸入數據。

      impairment, a portion of the carrying amount of a corporate                  如總部大樓）賬面值的一部分可按合理

      generating units.

      specific to the asset. An impairment loss is charged to profit or            生期間與該減值資產功能一致的開支類

      including transaction costs. Subsequent to initial recognition,               本）計 量。於 初 始 確 認 後，投 資 物 業 按

      method.

      of the property over the estimated useful lives of 35 years using             年以直線法撇銷該物業的成本。
      the straight-line method.

      The Group’s business model for managing financial assets refers              本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其
      to how it manages its financial assets in order to generate cash             如何管理其金融資產以產生現金流量。
      flows. The business model determines whether cash flows                      業務模式確定現金流量是否來自收取合
      will result from collecting contractual cash flows, selling the              約現金流量、出售金融資產，或兩者兼
      financial assets, or both. Financial assets classified and measured          有。按攤銷成本分類及計量的金融資產
      at amortised cost are held within a business model with the                  乃於旨在持有金融資產以收取合約現金
      objective to hold financial assets in order to collect contractual           流量的業務模式中持有，而按公允值計
      cash flows, while financial assets classified and measured at                入其他全面收入分類及計量的金融資產
      fair value through other comprehensive income are held within                則於旨在持有金融資產以收取合約現金
      a business model with the objective of both holding to collect               流量及出售的業務模式中持有。並非於
      contractual cash flows and selling. Financial assets which are not           上述業務模式中持有的金融資產按公允
      held within the aforementioned business models are classified                值計入損益分類及計量。
      and measured at fair value through profit or loss.


      Purchases or sales of financial assets that require delivery of              購買或出售須於一般由市場規則或慣例
      assets within the period generally established by regulation or              規定的期限內交付資產的金融資產於交
      convention in the marketplace are recognised on the trade date,              易日（即本集團承諾購買或出售該資產
      that is, the date that the Group commits to purchase or sell the             當日）確認。
      asset.

      when the asset is derecognised, modified or impaired.                        益及虧損於損益確認。

            Derecognition of financial assets                                        終止確認金融資產

            A financial asset (or, where applicable, a part of a financial           金融資產（或（如適用）一項金融資產的
            asset or part of a group of similar financial assets) is primarily       一部分或一組類似金融資產組別的一部
            derecognised (i.e., removed from the Group’s consolidated                分）主要在下列情況下終止確認（即自本
            statement of financial position) when:                                   集團的綜合財務狀況表中移除）：


            •      the rights to receive cash flows from the asset have              •   收取該資產所得現金流量的權利
                   expired; or                                                           已屆滿；或


            •      the Group has transferred its rights to receive cash flows        •   本集團已轉讓其收取該資產所得
                   from the asset or has assumed an obligation to pay the                現 金 流 量 的 權 利，或 根 據「轉 付」
                   received cash flows in full without material delay to a third         安排承擔在無嚴重延遲的情況下
                   party under a “pass-through” arrangement; and either                  向第三方全數支付所收取的現金
                   (a) the Group has transferred substantially all the risks             流 量 的 責 任；及 (a) 本 集 團 已 轉 讓
                   and rewards of the asset, or (b) the Group has neither                該 資 產 的 絕 大 部 分 風 險 及 回 報，
                   transferred nor retained substantially all the risks and              或 (b) 本集團概無轉讓或保留該資
                   rewards of the asset, but has transferred control of the              產 的 絕 大 部 分 風 險 及 回 報，但 已
                   asset.                                                                轉讓該資產的控制權。


            When the Group has transferred its rights to receive cash flows          倘本集團已轉讓其收取一項資產所得現
            from an asset or has entered into a pass-through arrangement,            金流量的權利或訂立轉付安排，則會評
            it evaluates if, and to what extent, it has retained the risk            估其是否及多大程度保留該資產擁有權
            and rewards of ownership of the asset. When it has neither               的風險及回報。倘其概無轉讓或保留該
            transferred nor retained substantially all the risks and rewards         資產的絕大部分風險及回報，亦無轉讓
            of the asset nor transferred control of the asset, the Group             該資產的控制權，本集團則按其持續參
            continues to recognise the transferred asset to the extent of            與資產的程度繼續確認已轉讓資產。在
            the Group’s continuing involvement. In that case, the Group              此情況下，本集團亦確認相關負債。已
            also recognises an associated liability. The transferred asset and       轉讓資產及相關負債按可反映本集團保
            the associated liability are measured on a basis that reflects the       留的權利及責任的基準計量。
            rights and obligations that the Group has retained.


            Continuing involvement that takes the form of a guarantee over           本集團倘以擔保形式持續參與已轉讓資

2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                2.4 重大會計政策（續）

      Impairment of financial assets                                               金融資產減值

      The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses                 本集團就並非按公允值計入損益持有的
      (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through             所 有 債 務 工 具 確 認 預 期 信 貸 虧 損（「預
      profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the                 期信貸虧損」）撥備。預期信貸虧損乃基
      contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract and               於根據合約應付的合約現金流量與本集
      all the cash flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted             團預期收取的所有現金流量之間的差額
      at an approximation of the original effective interest rate. The             釐定，並按原有實際利率相近之利率貼
      expected cash flows will include cash flows from the sale of                 現。預期現金流量將包括出售所持抵押
      collateral held or other credit enhancements that are integral to            品或構成合約條款組成部分的其他信貸
      the contractual terms.                                                       提升措施所得的現金流量。


      General approach                                                             一般方法


      ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for                  預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自初
      which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk               始確認以來未有顯著增加的信貸風險而
      since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses that          言，會就未來 12 個月可能發生的違約事
      result from default events that are possible within the next                 件所產生的信貸虧損計提預期信貸虧損
      12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for                   撥 備（ 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損）。就 自 初
      which there has been a significant increase in credit risk since             始確認以來顯著增加的信貸風險而言，
      initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit losses          不論違約事件於何時發生，須就預期於
      expected over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of            風險餘下存續期內產生的信貸虧損計提
      the timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).                                  虧損撥備（整個存續期預期信貸虧損）。


      At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the credit                於各報告日期，本集團評估自初始確認
      risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly since             以來金融工具的信貸風險是否顯著增
      initial recognition. When making the assessment, the Group                   加。作此評估時，本集團會對金融工具
      compares the risk of a default occurring on the financial                    於報告日期出現違約的風險與該金融工
      instrument as at the reporting date with the risk of a default               具於初始確認日期出現違約的風險進行
      occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of initial              比較，並考慮無須付出不必要成本或精
      recognition and considers reasonable and supportable                         力即可獲得的合理且具理據的資料，包
      information that is available without undue cost or effort,                  括過往及前瞻性資料。本集團認為，倘
      including historical and forward-looking information. The Group              合約付款逾期超過 30 天，信貸風險即顯
      considers that there has been a significant increase in credit risk          著增加。
      when contractual payments are more than 30 days past due.

            Derecognition of financial liabilities                                   終止確認金融負債

            A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under          金融負債於負債項下的責任解除、取消
            the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.                    或屆滿時終止確認。


            When an existing financial liability is replaced by another from the     倘現有金融負債由同一貸款人以大致上
            same lender on substantially different terms, or the terms of an         不同條款的另一金融負債所取代，或倘
            existing liability are substantially modified, such an exchange or       現有負債的條款被大幅修訂，該交換或
            modification is treated as a derecognition of the original liability     修訂被視為終止確認原有負債及確認新
            and a recognition of a new liability, and the difference between         負債處理，而有關賬面值的差額於損益
            the respective carrying amounts is recognised in profit or loss.         內確認。


            Offsetting of financial instruments                                      抵銷金融工具

            Financial assets and financial liabilities are offset and the net        倘現時有可予執行的法律權利抵銷已確
            amount is reported in the statement of financial position if there       認金額，且有意按淨額基準結算或同時
            is a currently enforceable legal right to offset the recognised          變現資產與清償負債，則可抵銷金融資
            amounts and there is an intention to settle on a net basis, or to        產及金融負債並於財務狀況表內呈報有
            realise the assets and settle the liabilities simultaneously.            關淨額。


            Cash and cash equivalents                                                現金及現金等價物

            Cash and cash equivalents in the statement of financial position         於財務狀況表內的現金及現金等價物包
            comprise cash on hand and at banks, and short-term highly                括手頭現金及銀行存款，以及一般在三
            liquid deposits with a maturity of generally within three months         個月內到期的高流動性短期存款，該等
            that are readily convertible into known amounts of cash, subject         存款可隨時兌換成已知金額的現金，價
            to an insignificant risk of changes in value and held for the            值變動風險不大，並且是為履行短期現
            purpose of meeting short-term cash commitments.                          金承諾而持有。


            For the purpose of the consolidated statement of cash flows,             就綜合現金流量表而言，現金及現金等
            cash and cash equivalents comprise cash on hand and at banks,            價物包括手頭現金及銀行存款，及上述
            and short-term deposits as defined above, less bank overdrafts           定義的短期存款，減須按要求償還並構
            which are repayable on demand and form an integral part of               成本集團現金管理組成部分的銀行透支。
            the Group’s cash management.


            Provisions                                                               撥備

            A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or            倘因過往事件導致現有責任（法定或推
            constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is           定），且 日 後 可 能 需 要 資 源 流 出 以 履 行
            probable that a future outflow of resources will be required to          有關責任，則確認撥備，惟責任所涉及
            settle the obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can be          金額須能可靠地予以估計。
            made of the amount of the obligation.

            it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profit will be          能有足夠應課稅溢利以動用全部或部分
            available to allow all or part of the deferred tax asset to be           遞延稅項資產為止。未被確認的遞延稅
            utilised. Unrecognised deferred tax assets are reassessed at the         項資產會於各報告期末重新評估，並在
            end of each reporting period and are recognised to the extent            可能有足夠應課稅溢利以收回全部或部
            that it has become probable that sufficient taxable profit will          分遞延稅項資產時予以確認。
            be available to allow all or part of the deferred tax asset to be
            recovered.


            Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax rates        遞延稅項資產及負債乃根據於各報告期
            that are expected to apply to the period when the asset is               末已頒佈或實質上已頒佈的稅率（及稅
            realised or the liability is settled, based on tax rates (and tax        法），按 預 期 適 用 於 變 現 資 產 或 清 還 負
            laws) that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the             債期間的稅率計算。
            end of the reporting period.

            At the end of the reporting period, the cash and bank balances     於報告期結束時，本集團以人民幣計值
            and time deposits of the Group amounting to approximately          的現金及銀行存款結餘和定期存款約為

            denominated in RMB. The RMB is not freely convertible into         人 民 幣 1,254,600,000 元）。人 民 幣 不 能
            other currencies, however, under the Chinese Mainland’s            自由兌換為其他貨幣。然而，根據中國
            Foreign Exchange Control Regulations and Administration            內地的《外匯管理條例》及《結匯、售匯
            of Settlement, and Sale and Payment of Foreign Exchange            及 付 匯 管 理 規 定》，本 集 團 可 透 過 獲 授
            Regulations, the Group is permitted to exchange RMB for            權開展外匯業務的銀行將人民幣兌換成

      therein for the year are presented in the consolidated statement           呈列於綜合權益變動表。
      of changes in equity.


      (a) Statutory surplus reserve                                              (a) 法定盈餘儲備

            In accordance with the PRC Company Law and the                             根據中國公司法及於中國成立的
            articles of association of the subsidiaries established in                 附 屬 公 司 的 組 織 章 程 細 則，各 相
            the PRC, each of the relevant subsidiaries is required to                  關附屬公司須按除稅後淨利潤的
            appropriate 10% of its net profit after tax, as determined                 10% 提 取 法 定 盈 餘 儲 備，此 乃 根
            under the Chinese Accounting Standards, to the statutory                   據 中 國 會 計 準 則 釐 定，直 至 儲 備
            surplus reserve until the reserve balance reaches 50% of                   餘 額 達 到 其 註 冊 資 本 的 50%。受
            its registered capital. Subject to certain restrictions set                相關中國法規及相關附屬公司組
            out in the relevant PRC regulations and in the articles                    織章程細則所載若干限制的規限，
            of association of the relevant subsidiaries, the statutory                 法定盈餘儲備可用於抵銷虧損或
            surplus reserve may be used either to offset losses, or to                 轉 增 股 本，惟 轉 換 後 餘 額 不 得 少
            be converted to increase the share capital provided that                   於 註 冊 資 本 的 25%。儲 備 不 得 用
            the balance after such conversion is not less than 25%                     作 其 設 立 目 的 以 外 的 其 他 用 途，
            of registered capital. The reserve cannot be used for                      亦不得作為現金股息分派。
            purposes other than those for which it is created and is
            not distributable as cash dividends.


      (b) Merger reserve                                                         (b) 合併儲備

            subsidiary pursuant to the reorganisation; and (ii) the                    附 屬 公 司 的 成 本；及 (ii) 於 重 組 完
            capital contributions from the China SCE Group prior to                    成前中駿集團的注資。
            the completion of the reorganisation.

28. SHARE-BASED PAYMENTS                                                 28. 以股份為基礎的付款

      On 2 March 2020, the Company issued and granted 900                       於二零二零年三月二日，本公司向裕威
      new ordinary shares as restricted shares to Affluent Way                  及 12 名僱員發行及授出 900 股新普通股
      and 12 employees at cash considerations of approximately                  作為受限制股份，現金代價分別約為人
      RMB149,381,000 (HK$166,500,000) and RMB24,898,000                         民幣 149,381,000 元（166,500,000 港元）
      (HK$27,750,000), respectively, which would be vested on the               及 人 民 幣 24,898,000 元（ 27,750,000 港
      condition that the employees remain in service for 5 years from           元），其 歸 屬 條 件 為 僱 員 將 於 授 出 日 期
      the date of grant. Accordingly, the Group measured the fair               起計五年內繼續任職。因此，本集團計

      Credit risk                                                                 信貸風險

      The Group is exposed to credit risk in relation to its trade                本集團面臨有關其貿易應收款項、其他
      receivables, other receivables, amount due from a related party             應收款項、應收關聯方款項以及現金及
      and cash and cash equivalents.                                              現金等價物的信貸風險。


      The Group expects that there is no significant credit risk                  由於現金及現金等價物主要存放於國有
      associated with cash and cash equivalents since they are                    銀行及其他中型或大型上市銀行，故本
      substantially placed at state-owned banks and other medium                  集團預期不會面臨有關現金及銀行結餘
      or large-sized listed banks. Management does not expect that                的重大信貸風險。管理層預期將不會因
      there will be any significant losses from non-performance by                該等交易對手方不履約而產生任何重大
      these counterparties.                                                       損失。


      The Group is exposed to credit risk in relation to its trade                本集團面臨與其貿易應收款項及應收關
      receivables and amount due from a related party. Information                聯方款項有關的信貸風險。有關確認本
      about the impairment provision recognised during the year is                年度減值撥備的詳情分別載於綜合財務
      disclosed in note 19 and 31(a), respectively to the consolidated            報表附註 19 及 31(a)。
      financial statements.


      The Group trades only with recognised and creditworthy third                本集團僅與獲認可及信譽良好的第三方
      parties. Concentrations of credit risk are managed by analysis by           進行交易，並通過分析客戶╱交易對手
      customer/counterparty. There are no significant concentrations              對集中信貸風險進行管理。由於本集團
      of credit risk within the Group as the customer bases of                    的來自第三方貿易應收款項及其他應收
      the Group’s trade receivables from third parties and other                  款項的客戶基礎廣泛分佈，因此本集團
      receivables are widely dispersed. In addition, receivable balances          並無重大集中信貸風險。此外，應收款
      are monitored on an ongoing basis.                                          項結餘會受到持續監控。
